As the surface warms, it can also warm the air nearby.\nShade changes how much sunlight reaches a surface. A tree canopy can block some sunlight before it reaches pavement. Trees can also release water vapor through their leaves. As water changes from liquid to vapor, the process can cool the surrounding area.\nSchools may add shade trees or shade structures, but planning matters. A young tree needs time, suitable soil, and care before it provides a wide canopy. A shade structure can provide a covered area sooner, though it does not offer all the benefits of a living tree. Combining approaches can help a school make comfortable outdoor spaces.",
